===== M0003 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2407-2417
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2407 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2408 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2409 |     int len = s.length();
2410 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2411 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2412 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2413 |         return false;
2414 |       }
2415 |     }
2416 |     return true;
2417 |   }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.canInline()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 280-406
Documentation:

Method code:
280 |     private boolean canInline() {
281 |       // Cannot inline a parameter.
282 |       if (getDefCfgNode().isFunction()) {
283 |         return false;
284 |       }
285 | 
286 |       // If one of our dependencies has been inlined, then our dependency
287 |       // graph is wrong. Re-computing it would take another CFG computation,
288 |       // so we just back off for now.
289 |       for (Var dependency : defMetadata.depends) {
290 |         if (inlinedNewDependencies.contains(dependency)) {
291 |           return false;
292 |         }
293 |       }
294 | 
295 |       getDefinition(getDefCfgNode(), null);
296 |       getNumUseInUseCfgNode(useCfgNode, null);
297 | 
298 |       // Definition was not found.
299 |       if (def == null) {
300 |         return false;
301 |       }
302 | 
303 |       // Check that the assignment isn't used as a R-Value.
304 |       // TODO(user): Certain cases we can still inline.
305 |       if (def.isAssign() && !NodeUtil.isExprAssign(def.getParent())) {
306 |         return false;
307 |       }
308 | 
309 |       // The right of the definition has side effect:
310 |       // Example, for x:
311 |       // x = readProp(b), modifyProp(b); print(x);
312 |       if (checkRightOf(def, getDefCfgNode(), SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE)) {
313 |         return false;
314 |       }
315 | 
316 |       // Similar check as the above but this time, all the sub-expressions
317 |       // left of the use of the variable.
318 |       // x = readProp(b); modifyProp(b), print(x);
319 |       if (checkLeftOf(use, useCfgNode, SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE)) {
320 |         return false;
321 |       }
322 | 
323 |       // TODO(user): Side-effect is OK sometimes. As long as there are no
324 |       // side-effect function down all paths to the use. Once we have all the
325 |       // side-effect analysis tool.
326 |       if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(def.getLastChild())) {
327 |         return false;
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       // TODO(user): We could inline all the uses if the expression is short.
331 | 
332 |       // Finally we have to make sure that there are no more than one use
333 |       // in the program and in the CFG node. Even when it is semantically
334 |       // correctly inlining twice increases code size.
335 |       if (numUseWithinUseCfgNode != 1) {
336 |         return false;
337 |       }
338 | 
339 |       // Make sure that the name is not within a loop
340 |       if (NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(use)) {
341 |         return false;
342 |       }
343 | 
344 | 
345 |       Collection<Node> uses = reachingUses.getUses(varName, getDefCfgNode());
346 | 
347 |       if (uses.size() != 1) {
348 |         return false;
349 |       }
350 | 
351 |       // We give up inlining stuff with R-Value that has GETPROP, GETELEM,
352 |       // or anything that creates a new object.
353 |       // Example:
354 |       // var x = a.b.c; j.c = 1; print(x);
355 |       // Inlining print(a.b.c) is not safe consider j and be alias to a.b.
356 |       // TODO(user): We could get more accuracy by looking more in-detail
357 |       // what j is and what x is trying to into to.
358 |       if (NodeUtil.has(def.getLastChild(),
359 |           new Predicate<Node>() {
360 |               @Override
361 |               public boolean apply(Node input) {
362 |                 switch (input.getType()) {
363 |                   case Token.GETELEM:
364 |                   case Token.GETPROP:
365 |                   case Token.ARRAYLIT:
366 |                   case Token.OBJECTLIT:
367 |                   case Token.REGEXP:
368 |                   case Token.NEW:
369 |                     return true;
370 |                 }
371 |                 return false;
372 |               }
373 |           },
374 |           new Predicate<Node>() {
375 |               @Override
376 |               public boolean apply(Node input) {
377 |                 // Recurse if the node is not a function.
378 |                 return !input.isFunction();
379 |               }
380 |           })) {
381 |         return false;
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       // We can skip the side effect check along the paths of two nodes if
385 |       // they are just next to each other.
386 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(getDefCfgNode().getParent()) &&
387 |           getDefCfgNode().getNext() != useCfgNode) {
388 |         // Similar side effect check as above but this time the side effect is
389 |         // else where along the path.
390 |         // x = readProp(b); while(modifyProp(b)) {}; print(x);
391 |         CheckPathsBetweenNodes<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>
392 |           pathCheck = new CheckPathsBetweenNodes<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(
393 |                  cfg,
394 |                  cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(getDefCfgNode()),
395 |                  cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(useCfgNode),
396 |                  SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE,
397 |                  Predicates.
398 |                      <DiGraphEdge<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>>alwaysTrue(),
399 |                  false);
400 |         if (pathCheck.somePathsSatisfyPredicate()) {
401 |           return false;
402 |         }
403 |       }
404 | 
405 |       return true;
406 |     }

75 |   @Override
76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used, the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
85 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
86 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
87 |     } else {
88 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     if (!declarationRoot.isFunction()) {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 619-631
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
619 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
620 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
621 |     if (typeSet == null) {
622 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
623 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
627 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
628 | 
629 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
630 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
631 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 640-672
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
640 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
641 |       Preconditions.checkState(
642 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
643 |       Node previous = null;
644 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
645 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
646 |       // are already in the right place.
647 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
648 |         previous = current;
649 |         current = current.getNext();
650 |       }
651 | 
652 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
653 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
654 |       while (current != null) {
655 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
656 |         Node next = current.getNext();
657 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
658 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
659 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
660 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
661 | 
662 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
663 |           // previous declarations).
664 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
665 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
666 |         } else {
667 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
668 |           previous = current;
669 |         }
670 |         current = next;
671 |       }
672 |     }

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 463-483
Documentation:

Method code:
463 |     @Override
464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
465 |       if (lineBreak) {
466 |         if (sawFunction) {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
473 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
474 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
475 |       int len = code.length();
476 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
477 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
478 |         if (ch == ';') {
479 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |       maybeCutLine();
483 |     }

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a FOR, DO, WHILE, WITH, or IF node.
   */
Method code:
1625 |   static boolean isControlStructure(Node n) {
1626 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1627 |       case Token.FOR:
1628 |       case Token.DO:
1629 |       case Token.WHILE:
1630 |       case Token.WITH:
1631 |       case Token.IF:
1632 |       case Token.LABEL:
1633 |       case Token.TRY:
1634 |       case Token.CATCH:
1635 |       case Token.SWITCH:
1636 |       case Token.CASE:
1637 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
1638 |         return true;
1639 |       default:
1640 |         return false;
1641 |     }
1642 |   }

/**
   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.
   */
Method code:
88 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
89 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
90 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
91 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
92 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);
93 |     for (String unrecognizedAnnotation : annotationWhitelist) {
94 |       if (!Annotation.recognizedAnnotations.containsKey(
95 |               unrecognizedAnnotation)) {
96 |         annotationBuilder.put(
97 |             unrecognizedAnnotation, Annotation.NOT_IMPLEMENTED);
98 |       }
99 |     }
100 |     return annotationBuilder.build();
101 |   }

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 773-787
Documentation:

Method code:
773 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
774 |     boolean valid = false;
775 |     if (i == 0) {
776 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
777 |     } else if (i == 1) {
778 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
779 |     } else {
780 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
781 |     }
782 |     if (!valid) {
783 |       violation(
784 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
785 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
786 |     }
787 |   }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.computeEscaped(Scope,Set<Var>,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 548-578
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute set of escaped variables. When a variable is escaped in a
   * dataflow analysis, it can be reference outside of the code that we are
   * analyzing. A variable is escaped if any of the following is true:
   *
   * <p><ol>
   * <li>It is defined as the exception name in CATCH clause so it became a
   * variable local not to our definition of scope.</li>
   * <li>Exported variables as they can be needed after the script terminates.
   * </li>
   * <li>Names of named functions because in JavaScript, <i>function foo(){}</i>
   * does not kill <i>foo</i> in the dataflow.</li>
   */
Method code:
548 |   static void computeEscaped(final Scope jsScope, final Set<Var> escaped,
549 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
550 |     // TODO(user): Very good place to store this information somewhere.
551 |     AbstractPostOrderCallback finder = new AbstractPostOrderCallback() {
552 |       @Override
553 |       public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
554 |         if (jsScope == t.getScope() || !n.isName()
555 |             || parent.isFunction()) {
556 |           return;
557 |         }
558 |         String name = n.getString();
559 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
560 |         if (var != null && var.scope == jsScope) {
561 |           escaped.add(jsScope.getVar(name));
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 |     };
565 | 
566 |     NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, finder);
567 |     t.traverseAtScope(jsScope);
568 | 
569 |     // 1: Remove the exception name in CATCH which technically isn't local to
570 |     //    begin with.
571 |     for (Iterator<Var> i = jsScope.getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
572 |       Var var = i.next();
573 |       if (var.getParentNode().isCatch() ||
574 |           compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.getName())) {
575 |         escaped.add(var);
576 |       }
577 |     }
578 |   }

323 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
324 |     if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
325 |       // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now. This avoids

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java

Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

===== M0113 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 274-337
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
274 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
275 |     this.options = options;
276 |     if (errorManager == null) {
277 |       if (outStream == null) {
278 |         setErrorManager(
279 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
280 |       } else {
281 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
282 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
283 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
284 |         setErrorManager(printer);
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
289 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
290 |       options.checkTypes = true;
291 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
292 |       options.checkTypes = false;
293 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
294 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
295 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
296 |       // parser type warnings.
297 |       options.setWarningLevel(
298 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
299 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
300 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
304 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
305 |       options.setWarningLevel(
306 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
307 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
311 |       options.setWarningLevel(
312 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
313 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
317 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
318 |     guards.add(
319 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
320 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
321 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
322 | 
323 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
324 | 
325 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
326 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
327 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
328 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
329 |     // error off.
330 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
331 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
332 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
333 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
337 |   }

===== M0119 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0133 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MustBeReachingVariableDef.computeMustDef(Node,Node,MustDef,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MustBeReachingVariableDef.java
Original range: 225-318
Documentation:
/**
   * @param n The node in question.
   * @param cfgNode The node to add
   * @param conditional true if the definition is not always executed.
   */
Method code:
225 |   private void computeMustDef(
226 |       Node n, Node cfgNode, MustDef output, boolean conditional) {
227 |     switch (n.getType()) {
228 | 
229 |       case Token.BLOCK:
230 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
231 |         return;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.WHILE:
234 |       case Token.DO:
235 |       case Token.IF:
236 |         computeMustDef(
237 |             NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
238 |         return;
239 | 
240 |       case Token.FOR:
241 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
242 |           computeMustDef(
243 |               NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
244 |         } else {
245 |           // for(x in y) {...}
246 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
247 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
248 |           if (lhs.isVar()) {
249 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild(); // for(var x in y) {...}
250 |           }
251 |           if (lhs.isName()) {
252 |             addToDefIfLocal(lhs.getString(), cfgNode, rhs, output);
253 |           }
254 |         }
255 |         return;
256 | 
257 |       case Token.AND:
258 |       case Token.OR:
259 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
260 |         computeMustDef(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.HOOK:
264 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
265 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), cfgNode, output, true);
266 |         computeMustDef(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
267 |         return;
268 | 
269 |       case Token.VAR:
270 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
271 |           if (c.hasChildren()) {
272 |             computeMustDef(c.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
273 |             addToDefIfLocal(c.getString(), conditional ? null : cfgNode,
274 |                 c.getFirstChild(), output);
275 |           }
276 |         }
277 |         return;
278 | 
279 |       default:
280 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n)) {
281 |           if (n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
282 |             Node name = n.getFirstChild();
283 |             computeMustDef(name.getNext(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
284 |             addToDefIfLocal(name.getString(), conditional ? null : cfgNode,
285 |               n.getLastChild(), output);
286 |             return;
287 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isGet(n.getFirstChild())) {
288 |             // Treat all assignments to arguments as redefining the
289 |             // parameters itself.
290 |             Node obj = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
291 |             if (obj.isName() && "arguments".equals(obj.getString())) {
292 |               // TODO(user): More accuracy can be introduced
293 |               // i.e. We know exactly what arguments[x] is if x is a constant
294 |               // number.
295 |               escapeParameters(output);
296 |             }
297 |           }
298 |         }
299 | 
300 |         if (n.isName() && "arguments".equals(n.getString())) {
301 |           escapeParameters(output);
302 |         }
303 | 
304 |         // DEC and INC actually defines the variable.
305 |         if (n.isDec() || n.isInc()) {
306 |           Node target = n.getFirstChild();
307 |           if (target.isName()) {
308 |             addToDefIfLocal(target.getString(),
309 |                 conditional ? null : cfgNode, null, output);
310 |             return;
311 |           }
312 |         }
313 | 
314 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
315 |           computeMustDef(c, cfgNode, output, conditional);
316 |         }
317 |     }
318 |   }

===== M0136 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 951-1010
Documentation:

Method code:
951 |   private void toString(
952 |       StringBuilder sb,
953 |       boolean printSource,
954 |       boolean printAnnotations,
955 |       boolean printType) {
956 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
957 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       sb.append(getString());
960 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
961 |       sb.append(' ');
962 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
963 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
964 |       // toStringTree.
965 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
966 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
967 |       } else {
968 |         sb.append(first.getString());
969 |       }
970 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
971 |       sb.append(' ');
972 |       sb.append(getDouble());
973 |     }
974 |     if (printSource) {
975 |       int lineno = getLineno();
976 |       if (lineno != -1) {
977 |         sb.append(' ');
978 |         sb.append(lineno);
979 |       }
980 |     }
981 | 
982 |     if (printAnnotations) {
983 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
984 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
985 |         int type = keys[i];
986 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
987 |         sb.append(" [");
988 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
989 |         sb.append(": ");
990 |         String value;
991 |         switch (type) {
992 |           default:
993 |             value = x.toString();
994 |             break;
995 |         }
996 |         sb.append(value);
997 |         sb.append(']');
998 |       }
999 |     }
1000 | 
1001 |     if (printType) {
1002 |       if (jsType != null) {
1003 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1004 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1005 |           sb.append(" : ");
1006 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1007 |         }
1008 |       }
1009 |     }
1010 |   }

===== M0154 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 489-506
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0166 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.inlineVariable()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 411-432
Documentation:
/**
     * Actual transformation.
     */
Method code:
411 |     private void inlineVariable() {
412 |       Node defParent = def.getParent();
413 |       Node useParent = use.getParent();
414 |       if (def.isAssign()) {
415 |         Node rhs = def.getLastChild();
416 |         rhs.detachFromParent();
417 |         // Oh yes! I have grandparent to remove this.
418 |         Preconditions.checkState(defParent.isExprResult());
419 |         while (defParent.getParent().isLabel()) {
420 |           defParent = defParent.getParent();
421 |         }
422 |         defParent.detachFromParent();
423 |         useParent.replaceChild(use, rhs);
424 |       } else if (defParent.isVar()) {
425 |         Node rhs = def.getLastChild();
426 |         def.removeChild(rhs);
427 |         useParent.replaceChild(use, rhs);
428 |       } else {
429 |         Preconditions.checkState(false, "No other definitions can be inlined.");
430 |       }
431 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
432 |     }

===== M0173 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 741-757
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
741 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
742 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
743 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
744 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
745 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
746 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
747 |     }
748 | 
749 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
750 | 
751 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
752 |     do {
753 |       current = current.getParent();
754 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
755 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
756 |     } while (current != n);
757 |   }

===== M0174 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.GatherCandiates.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 197-244
Documentation:

Method code:
197 |     @Override
198 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
199 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> graphNode = cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(n);
200 |       if (graphNode == null) {
201 |         // Not a CFG node.
202 |         return;
203 |       }
204 |       FlowState<MustDef> state = graphNode.getAnnotation();
205 |       final MustDef defs = state.getIn();
206 |       final Node cfgNode = n;
207 |       AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback gatherCb =
208 |           new AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback() {
209 | 
210 |         @Override
211 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
212 |           if (n.isName()) {
213 | 
214 |             // n.getParent() isn't null. This just the case where n is the root
215 |             // node that gatherCb started at.
216 |             if (parent == null) {
217 |               return;
218 |             }
219 | 
220 |             // Make sure that the name node is purely a read.
221 |             if ((NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
222 |                 || parent.isVar() || parent.isInc() || parent.isDec() ||
223 |                 parent.isParamList() || parent.isCatch()) {
224 |               return;
225 |             }
226 | 
227 |             String name = n.getString();
228 |             if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(name)) {
229 |               return;
230 |             }
231 | 
232 |             Definition def = reachingDef.getDef(name, cfgNode);
233 |             // TODO(nicksantos): We need to add some notion of @const outer
234 |             // scope vars. We can inline those just fine.
235 |             if (def != null &&
236 |                 !reachingDef.dependsOnOuterScopeVars(def)) {
237 |               candidates.add(new Candidate(name, def, n, cfgNode));
238 |             }
239 |           }
240 |         }
241 |       };
242 | 
243 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, cfgNode, gatherCb);
244 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Given an expression by its root and sub-expression n, return true if there
   * the predicate is true for some expression on the right of n.
   *
   * Example:
   *
   * NotChecked(), NotChecked(), n, Checked(), Checked();
   */
Method code:
496 |   private static boolean checkRightOf(
497 |       Node n, Node expressionRoot, Predicate<Node> predicate) {
498 |     for (Node p = n; p != expressionRoot; p = p.getParent()) {
499 |       for (Node cur = p.getNext(); cur != null; cur = cur.getNext()) {
500 |         if (predicate.apply(cur)) {
501 |           return true;
502 |         }
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return false;
506 |   }

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 235-266
Documentation:

Method code:
235 |     @Override
236 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
237 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
238 |       if (n.isName()) {
239 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 | 
243 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
244 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top-level variable.
245 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
246 |         if (var != null) {
247 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
248 |         }
249 | 
250 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
251 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
252 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
253 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
254 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
255 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
256 |           if (assertOnChange) {
257 |             String name = n.getString();
258 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
259 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
260 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
261 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
262 |           }
263 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
264 |         }
265 |       }
266 |     }

===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and

   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.

   * @param associatedNode The source node containing the JSDoc.

177 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,

184 | 
185 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {

===== M0239 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 504-524
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
504 |   @Override
505 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
506 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
507 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
508 |     if (unknown) {
509 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
510 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
511 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
512 |         unknown = false;
513 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
514 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
515 |             unknown = true;
516 |             break;
517 |           }
518 |         }
519 |       } else {
520 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |     return unknown;
524 |   }

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

/**
   * Performs a single flow through a node.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if the flow state differs from the previous state.
   */
Method code:
267 |   protected boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
268 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
269 |     if (isForward()) {
270 |       L outBefore = state.out;
271 |       state.out = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
272 |       return !outBefore.equals(state.out);
273 |     } else {
274 |       L inBefore = state.in;
275 |       state.in = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.out);
276 |       return !inBefore.equals(state.in);
277 |     }
278 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1511-1560
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1511 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1512 |     if (type != node.getType()
1513 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1514 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1515 |       return false;
1516 |     }
1517 | 
1518 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1519 |       return false;
1520 |     }
1521 | 
1522 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1523 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1524 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1525 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1526 |         return false;
1527 |       }
1528 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1529 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1530 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1531 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1532 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1533 |           return false;
1534 |         }
1535 |       }
1536 | 
1537 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1538 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1539 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1540 |         return false;
1541 |       }
1542 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1543 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1544 |         return false;
1545 |       }
1546 |     }
1547 | 
1548 |     if (recurse) {
1549 |       Node n, n2;
1550 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1551 |            n != null;
1552 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1553 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1554 |           return false;
1555 |         }
1556 |       }
1557 |     }
1558 | 
1559 |     return true;
1560 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 182-951
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
182 |   boolean parse() {
183 |     int lineno;
184 |     int charno;
185 | 
186 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
187 |     JSTypeExpression type;
188 | 
189 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
190 |     skipEOLs();
191 | 
192 |     JsDocToken token = next();
193 | 
194 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
195 | 
196 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
197 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
198 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
199 |       token = blockInfo.token;
200 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
201 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
202 |       }
203 |     } else {
204 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
205 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
206 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
207 |         // what it was.
208 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
213 |     retry: for (;;) {
214 |       switch (token) {
215 |         case ANNOTATION:
216 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
217 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
218 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
219 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
220 | 
221 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
222 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
223 |             if (annotation == null) {
224 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |             } else {
227 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
228 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
229 | 
230 |               switch (annotation) {
231 |                 case AUTHOR:
232 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
233 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
234 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
235 | 
236 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
237 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
238 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                     } else {
240 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
241 |                     }
242 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
243 |                   } else {
244 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
245 |                   }
246 |                   continue retry;
247 | 
248 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
249 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
250 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
251 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case CONSTANT:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }
261 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
262 |                   continue retry;
263 | 
264 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
265 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
266 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
267 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
268 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
269 |                     } else {
270 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
271 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
272 |                     }
273 |                   }
274 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
275 |                   continue retry;
276 | 
277 |                 case DEPRECATED:
278 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
279 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
280 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
281 |                   }
282 | 
283 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
284 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
285 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
286 | 
287 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
288 | 
289 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
290 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
291 |                   }
292 | 
293 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
294 |                   continue retry;
295 | 
296 |                 case INTERFACE:
297 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
298 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
299 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
300 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
301 |                     } else {
302 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
303 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
304 |                     }
305 |                   }
306 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
307 |                   continue retry;
308 | 
309 |                 case DESC:
310 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
311 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
312 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
313 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
314 |                     continue retry;
315 |                   } else {
316 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
317 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
318 | 
319 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
320 | 
321 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
322 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
323 |                     continue retry;
324 |                   }
325 | 
326 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
327 |                   String fileOverview = "";
328 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
329 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
330 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
331 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
332 | 
333 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
334 | 
335 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
336 |                   } else {
337 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
338 |                   }
339 | 
340 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview)) {
341 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
342 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |                   }
344 |                   continue retry;
345 | 
346 |                 case LICENSE:
347 |                 case PRESERVE:
348 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
349 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
350 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
351 | 
352 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
353 | 
354 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
355 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
356 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
357 |                     }
358 |                   }
359 | 
360 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
361 |                   continue retry;
362 | 
363 |                 case ENUM:
364 |                   token = next();
365 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
366 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
367 | 
368 |                   type = null;
369 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
370 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
371 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));

375 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
376 |                   }
377 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
378 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
379 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
380 |                   }
381 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
382 |                   continue retry;
383 | 
384 |                 case EXPORT:
385 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
386 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
387 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
388 |                   }
389 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
390 |                   continue retry;
391 | 
392 |                 case EXPOSE:
393 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExpose()) {
394 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.expose",
395 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
396 |                   }
397 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
398 |                   continue retry;
399 | 
400 |                 case EXTERNS:
401 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
402 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
403 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
404 |                   }
405 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();

467 |                   }

492 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                     }

496 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
497 |                   }

605 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));

609 |                       // recovering parsing

611 |                       continue retry;
612 |                     }

614 |                     token = next();
615 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
616 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
617 |                   }

620 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
621 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
622 |                     token = next();
623 |                   }

626 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
627 |                         lineno, charno);

638 |                         token = next();

648 |                         // already.

843 |                     }

849 |                     // recovering parsing

854 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
855 |                               lineno, charno);
856 |                         }

858 | 
859 |                       case RETURN:

861 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
862 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
863 |                           break;
864 |                         }

869 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
870 | 
871 |                           String returnDescription =
872 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
873 | 
874 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
875 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
876 |                                 returnDescription);
877 |                           }
878 | 
879 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
880 |                         } else {
881 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
882 |                         }
883 |                         continue retry;
884 | 
885 |                       case THIS:
886 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
887 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
888 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
889 |                         }
890 |                         break;
891 | 
892 |                       case TYPE:
893 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
894 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
895 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
896 |                         }
897 |                         break;
898 | 
899 |                       case TYPEDEF:
900 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
901 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
902 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
903 |                         }
904 |                         break;
905 |                     }
906 |                   }
907 | 
908 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
909 |                   continue retry;
910 |               }
911 |             }
912 |           }
913 |           break;
914 | 
915 |         case EOC:
916 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
917 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
918 |           }
919 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
920 |           return true;
921 | 
922 |         case EOF:
923 |           // discard any accumulated information
924 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
925 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
926 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
927 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
928 |           return false;
929 | 
930 |         case EOL:
931 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
933 |           }
934 |           token = next();
935 |           continue retry;
936 | 
937 |         default:
938 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
939 |             token = next();
940 |             continue retry;
941 |           } else {
942 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
943 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
944 |             continue retry;
945 |           }
946 |       }
947 | 
948 |       // next token
949 |       token = next();
950 |     }
951 |   }

   *

===== M0297 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }
