===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-732
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // Unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         cc.addConstant("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         cc.addConstant("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         cc.addConstant("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
632 |         Preconditions.checkState(
633 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
634 |         addJsString(n);
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.STRING:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(
639 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
640 |         addJsString(n);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.DELPROP:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add("delete ");
646 |         add(first);
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
650 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
651 |         if (needsParens) {
652 |           add("(");
653 |         }
654 |         add("{");
655 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
656 |           if (c != first) {
657 |             cc.listSeparator();
658 |           }
659 | 
660 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
661 |             add(c);
662 |           } else {
663 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
664 |             String key = c.getString();
665 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
666 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
667 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
668 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
669 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
670 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
671 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
672 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
673 |               add(key);
674 |             } else {
675 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
676 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
677 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
678 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
679 |               } else {
680 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
681 |               }
682 |             }
683 |             add(":");
684 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
685 |           }
686 |         }
687 |         add("}");
688 |         if (needsParens) {
689 |           add(")");
690 |         }
691 |         break;
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       case Token.SWITCH:
695 |         add("switch(");
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(")");
698 |         cc.beginBlock();
699 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
700 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.CASE:
704 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
705 |         add("case ");
706 |         add(first);
707 |         addCaseBody(last);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
712 |         add("default");
713 |         addCaseBody(first);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.LABEL:
717 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
718 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
719 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
720 |         }
721 |         add(first);
722 |         add(":");
723 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
724 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
725 |         break;
726 | 
727 |       default:
728 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
732 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

210 |         @Override
211 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
212 |           if (n.isName()) {
213 | 
214 |             // n.getParent() isn't null. This just the case where n is the root
215 |             // node that gatherCb started at.
216 |             if (parent == null) {
217 |               return;
218 |             }
219 | 
220 |             // Make sure that the name node is purely a read.
221 |             if ((NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
222 |                 || parent.isVar() || parent.isInc() || parent.isDec() ||
223 |                 parent.isParamList() || parent.isCatch()) {
224 |               return;
225 |             }
226 |

228 |             if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(name)) {
229 |               return;
230 |             }

233 |             // TODO(nicksantos): We need to add some notion of @const outer
234 |             // scope vars. We can inline those just fine.
235 |             if (def != null &&

===== M0316 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1168-1241
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1168 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1169 |     switch (type) {
1170 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1183 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1184 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1185 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1186 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1187 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1188 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1189 |       case Token.EQ:
1190 |       case Token.NE:
1191 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1192 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1193 |       case Token.LT:
1194 |       case Token.GT:
1195 |       case Token.LE:
1196 |       case Token.GE:
1197 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1198 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1199 |       case Token.LSH:
1200 |       case Token.RSH:
1201 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1202 |       case Token.SUB:
1203 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1204 |       case Token.MUL:
1205 |       case Token.MOD:
1206 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1207 |       case Token.INC:
1208 |       case Token.DEC:
1209 |       case Token.NEW:
1210 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1211 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1212 |       case Token.VOID:
1213 |       case Token.NOT:
1214 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1215 |       case Token.POS:
1216 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1217 | 
1218 |       case Token.CALL:
1219 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1220 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1221 |       // Data values
1222 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1223 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1224 |       case Token.FALSE:
1225 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1226 |       case Token.NAME:
1227 |       case Token.NULL:
1228 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1229 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1230 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1231 |       case Token.STRING:
1232 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
1233 |       case Token.THIS:
1234 |       case Token.TRUE:
1235 |         return 15;
1236 | 
1237 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1238 |                                Token.name(type) +
1239 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1240 |     }
1241 |   }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0326 =====

381 |   public <T extends SourceFile> void initModules(

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckPathsBetweenNodes.discoverBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckPathsBetweenNodes.java
Original range: 139-153
Documentation:

Method code:
139 |   private void discoverBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E> u) {
140 |     u.setAnnotation(GRAY);
141 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> e : u.getOutEdges()) {
142 |       if (ignoreEdge(e)) {
143 |         continue;
144 |       }
145 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> v = e.getDestination();
146 |       if (v.getAnnotation() == WHITE) {
147 |         discoverBackEdges(v);
148 |       } else if (v.getAnnotation() == GRAY) {
149 |         e.setAnnotation(BACK_EDGE);
150 |       }
151 |     }
152 |     u.setAnnotation(BLACK);
153 |   }

Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
596 |   public Scope getScope() {
597 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
598 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
599 |       return scope;
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
603 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
604 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
605 |       scopes.push(scope);
606 |     }
607 |     scopeRoots.clear();
608 | 
609 |     return scope;
610 |   }

===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

156 |   @Override
157 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType that) {
158 |     if (this == that) {
159 |       return true;
160 |     } else if (this.isNominalType()) {
161 |       ObjectType thatObj = ObjectType.cast(that);
162 |       if (thatObj != null && thatObj.isNominalType()) {
163 |         return getReferenceName().equals(thatObj.getReferenceName());
164 |       }

167 |   }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)

Original range: 484-493
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
484 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
485 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
486 |          current != null;
487 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
488 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
489 |         return true;
490 |       }
491 |     }
492 |     return false;
493 |   }

===== M0363 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.checkLeftOf(Node,Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 516-527
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an expression by its root and sub-expression n, return true if there
   * the predicate is true for some expression on the left of n.
   *
   * Example:
   *
   * Checked(), Checked(), n, NotChecked(), NotChecked();
   */
Method code:
516 |   private static boolean checkLeftOf(
517 |       Node n, Node expressionRoot, Predicate<Node> predicate) {
518 |     for (Node p = n.getParent(); p != expressionRoot; p = p.getParent()) {
519 |       for (Node cur = p.getParent().getFirstChild(); cur != p;
520 |           cur = cur.getNext()) {
521 |         if (predicate.apply(cur)) {
522 |           return true;
523 |         }
524 |       }
525 |     }
526 |     return false;
527 |   }

468 |     private void getNumUseInUseCfgNode(Node n, Node parant) {

380 |     @Override
381 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
382 |       switch (n.getType()) {
383 |         case Token.WHILE:
384 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MustBeReachingVariableDef.MustDefJoin.apply(MustDef,MustDef)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MustBeReachingVariableDef.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |     @Override
149 |     public MustDef apply(MustDef a, MustDef b) {
150 |       MustDef result = new MustDef();
151 |       Map<Var, Definition> resultMap = result.reachingDef;
152 | 
153 |       // Take the join of all variables that are not TOP in this.
154 |       for (Map.Entry<Var, Definition> varEntry : a.reachingDef.entrySet()) {
155 |         Var var = varEntry.getKey();
156 |         Definition aDef = varEntry.getValue();
157 | 
158 |         if (aDef == null) {
159 |           // "a" is BOTTOM implies that the variable has more than one possible
160 |           // definition. We set the join of this to be BOTTOM regardless of what
161 |           // "b" might be.
162 |           resultMap.put(var, null);
163 |           continue;
164 |         }
165 | 
166 |         Node aNode = aDef.node;
167 | 
168 |         if (b.reachingDef.containsKey(var)) {
169 |           Definition bDef = b.reachingDef.get(var);
170 | 
171 |           if (aDef.equals(bDef)) {
172 |             resultMap.put(var, aDef);
173 |           } else {
174 |             resultMap.put(var, null);
175 |           }
176 |         } else {
177 |           resultMap.put(var, aDef);
178 |         }
179 |       }
180 | 
181 |       // Take the join of all variables that are not TOP in other but it is TOP
182 |       // in this.
183 |       for (Map.Entry<Var, Definition> entry : b.reachingDef.entrySet()) {
184 |         Var var = entry.getKey();
185 |         if (!a.reachingDef.containsKey(var)) {
186 |           resultMap.put(var, entry.getValue());
187 |         }
188 |       }
189 |       return result;
190 |     }

===== M0404 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getSortedPropTypes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
881 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
882 |     int count = 0;
883 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
884 |       count++;
885 |     }
886 | 
887 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
888 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
889 |       count--;
890 |       keys[count] = x.getType();
891 |     }
892 | 
893 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
894 |     return keys;
895 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1570-1585
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1570 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1571 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1572 |       String name = getString();
1573 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1574 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1575 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1576 |       if (left == null) {
1577 |         return null;
1578 |       }
1579 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1580 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1581 |       return "this";
1582 |     } else {
1583 |       return null;
1584 |     }
1585 |   }

===== M0434 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1280-1402
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1280 |   Node parseInputs() {
1281 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1282 | 
1283 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1284 |     // individual file parse trees.
1285 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1286 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1287 |     }
1288 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1289 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1290 |     }
1291 | 
1292 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1293 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1294 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1295 | 
1296 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1297 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1298 | 
1299 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1300 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1301 | 
1302 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1303 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1304 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1305 |     }
1306 | 
1307 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1308 | 
1309 |     try {
1310 |       // Parse externs sources.
1311 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1312 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1313 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1314 |           return null;
1315 |         }
1316 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1317 |       }
1318 | 
1319 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1320 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1321 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1322 |       }
1323 | 
1324 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1325 | 
1326 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1327 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1328 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1329 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1330 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1331 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1332 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1333 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1334 |           }
1335 |         }
1336 | 
1337 |         try {
1338 |           inputs =
1339 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1340 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1341 |           staleInputs = true;
1342 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1343 |           report(JSError.make(
1344 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1345 | 
1346 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1347 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1348 |             return null;
1349 |           }
1350 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1351 |           report(JSError.make(
1352 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1353 | 
1354 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1355 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1356 |             return null;
1357 |           }
1358 |         }
1359 |       }
1360 | 
1361 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1362 | 
1363 |       if (staleInputs) {
1364 |         repartitionInputs();
1365 |       }
1366 | 
1367 |       // Build the AST.
1368 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1369 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1370 |         if (n == null) {
1371 |           continue;
1372 |         }
1373 | 
1374 |         if (devMode) {
1375 |           runSanityCheck();
1376 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1377 |             return null;
1378 |           }
1379 |         }
1380 | 
1381 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1382 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1383 | 
1384 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1385 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1386 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1387 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1388 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1389 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1390 |         }
1391 | 
1392 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1393 |       }
1394 | 
1395 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1396 |         return null;
1397 |       }
1398 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1399 |     } finally {
1400 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1401 |     }
1402 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1080-1108
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1080 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1081 |     // unknown
1082 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1083 |       return true;
1084 |     }
1085 |     // equality
1086 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1087 |       return true;
1088 |     }
1089 |     // all type
1090 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1091 |       return true;
1092 |     }
1093 |     // unions
1094 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1095 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1096 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1097 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1098 |           return true;
1099 |         }
1100 |       }
1101 |     }
1102 |     // proxy types
1103 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1104 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1105 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1106 |     }
1107 |     return false;
1108 |   }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 286-324
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the new flow state at a given node's entry by merging the
   * output (input) lattice of the node's predecessor (successor).
   *
   * @param node Node to compute new join.
   */
Method code:
286 |   protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
287 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
288 |     if (isForward()) {
289 |       if (cfg.getEntry() == node) {
290 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
291 |       } else {
292 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(node);
293 |         if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
294 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNodes.get(0).getAnnotation();
295 |           state.setIn(inNodeState.getOut());
296 |         } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
297 |           List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
298 |           for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
299 |             FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
300 |             values.add(currentNodeState.getOut());
301 |           }
302 |           state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |     } else {
306 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(node);
307 |       if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
308 |         DiGraphNode<N, Branch> inNode = inNodes.get(0);
309 |         if (inNode == cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
310 |           state.setOut(createEntryLattice());
311 |         } else {
312 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNode.getAnnotation();
313 |           state.setOut(inNodeState.getIn());
314 |         }
315 |       } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
316 |         List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
317 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
318 |           FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
319 |           values.add(currentNodeState.getIn());
320 |         }
321 |         state.setOut(joinOp.apply(values));
322 |       }
323 |     }
324 |   }

522 |       case Token.WITH:

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows

Method code:
555 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
556 |     if (child == first) {
557 |       return null;
558 |     }
559 |     Node n = first;
560 |     while (n.next != child) {
561 |       n = n.next;
562 |       if (n == null) {
563 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
564 |       }
565 |     }
566 |     return n;
567 |   }

===== M0477 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MaybeReachingVariableUse.computeMayUse(Node,Node,ReachingUses,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MaybeReachingVariableUse.java
Original range: 163-248
Documentation:

Method code:
163 |   private void computeMayUse(
164 |       Node n, Node cfgNode, ReachingUses output, boolean conditional) {
165 |     switch (n.getType()) {
166 | 
167 |       case Token.BLOCK:
168 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
169 |         return;
170 | 
171 |       case Token.NAME:
172 |         addToUseIfLocal(n.getString(), cfgNode, output);
173 |         return;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         computeMayUse(
179 |             NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
180 |         return;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
184 |           computeMayUse(
185 |               NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
186 |         } else {
187 |           // for(x in y) {...}
188 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
189 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
190 |           if (lhs.isVar()) {
191 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild(); // for(var x in y) {...}
192 |           }
193 |           if (lhs.isName() && !conditional) {
194 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(lhs.getString(), output);
195 |           }
196 |           computeMayUse(rhs, cfgNode, output, conditional);
197 |         }
198 |         return;
199 | 
200 |       case Token.AND:
201 |       case Token.OR:
202 |         computeMayUse(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
203 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       case Token.HOOK:
207 |         computeMayUse(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
208 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), cfgNode, output, true);
209 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
210 |         return;
211 | 
212 |       case Token.VAR:
213 |         Node varName = n.getFirstChild();
214 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasChildren(), "AST should be normalized");
215 | 
216 |         if (varName.hasChildren()) {
217 |           computeMayUse(varName.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
218 |           if (!conditional) {
219 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(varName.getString(), output);
220 |           }
221 |         }
222 |         return;
223 | 
224 |       default:
225 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
226 |           Node name = n.getFirstChild();
227 |           if (!conditional) {
228 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(name.getString(), output);
229 |           }
230 | 
231 |           // In case of a += "Hello". There is a read of a.
232 |           if (!n.isAssign()) {
233 |             addToUseIfLocal(name.getString(), cfgNode, output);
234 |           }
235 | 
236 |           computeMayUse(name.getNext(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
237 |         } else {
238 |           /*
239 |            * We want to traverse in reverse order because we want the LAST
240 |            * definition in the sub-tree....
241 |            * But we have no better way to traverse in reverse other :'(
242 |            */
243 |           for (Node c = n.getLastChild(); c != null; c = n.getChildBefore(c)) {
244 |             computeMayUse(c, cfgNode, output, conditional);
245 |           }
246 |         }
247 |     }
248 |   }

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistNoCompileFiles()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1449-1469
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompiler annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1449 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1450 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1451 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1452 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1453 | 
1454 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1455 |       if (n == null) {
1456 |         continue;
1457 |       }
1458 | 
1459 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1460 |       if (info != null && info.isNoCompile()) {
1461 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1462 |         staleInputs = true;
1463 |       }
1464 |     }
1465 | 
1466 |     if (staleInputs) {
1467 |       repartitionInputs();
1468 |     }
1469 |   }

===== M0495 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1104-1124
Documentation:

Method code:
1104 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1105 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1106 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1107 |       return s;
1108 |     }
1109 | 
1110 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1111 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1112 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1113 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1114 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1115 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1116 |       // crazy.
1117 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1118 |         sb.append(c);
1119 |       } else {
1120 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1121 |       }
1122 |     }
1123 |     return sb.toString();
1124 |   }

===== M0501 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.

175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

===== M0520 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1910-1912
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1910 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1911 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1912 |   }

===== M0525 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 994-1050
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
994 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
995 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
996 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
997 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
998 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
999 |     }
1000 | 
1001 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
1002 |       return false;
1003 |     }
1004 | 
1005 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
1006 | 
1007 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1008 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1009 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1010 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1011 |         return false;
1012 |       }
1013 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1014 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1015 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1016 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1017 |         return false;
1018 |       }
1019 | 
1020 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1021 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1022 |         return false;
1023 |       }
1024 | 
1025 |       // Math.floor has no side-effects.
1026 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1027 |       // I create a definitionProvider that understands namespacing.
1028 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1029 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1030 |           return false;
1031 |         }
1032 |       }
1033 | 
1034 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1035 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1036 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1037 |           return false;
1038 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1039 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1040 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1041 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1042 |           if (param != null &&
1043 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp()))
1044 |           return false;
1045 |         }
1046 |       }
1047 |     }
1048 | 
1049 |     return true;
1050 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)

Original range: 617-634
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
617 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
618 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
619 |         next = c.getNext();
620 |         if (c.isVar()) {
621 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
622 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
623 |           }
624 | 
625 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
626 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
627 |             c.removeChild(name);
628 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
629 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
630 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
631 |           }
632 |         }
633 |       }
634 |     }

===== M0532 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0554 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 345-371
Documentation:

Method code:
345 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
346 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
347 |     // two cases:
348 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
349 |     // and
350 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
351 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
352 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
353 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
354 |     //
355 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
356 |     //
357 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
358 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
359 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
360 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
361 |     //
362 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
363 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
364 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
365 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
366 | 
367 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
368 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
369 |     }
370 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
371 |   }

===== M0560 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseAtScope(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 351-372
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively with a scope, starting at that scope's
   * root.
   */
Method code:
351 |   void traverseAtScope(Scope s) {
352 |     Node n = s.getRootNode();
353 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
354 |       // We need to do some extra magic to make sure that the scope doesn't
355 |       // get re-created when we dive into the function.
356 |       if (inputId == null) {
357 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
358 |       }
359 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
360 |       curNode = n;
361 |       pushScope(s);
362 | 
363 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
364 |       Node body = args.getNext();
365 |       traverseBranch(args, n);
366 |       traverseBranch(body, n);
367 | 
368 |       popScope();
369 |     } else {
370 |       traverseWithScope(n, s);
371 |     }
372 |   }
