===== M0574 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.ON_EX, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0585 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3011-3037
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3011 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3012 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3013 |     if (info == null) {
3014 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3015 |       if (parent == null) {
3016 |         return null;
3017 |       }
3018 | 
3019 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3020 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3021 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3022 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3024 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3025 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3026 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3027 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3028 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3029 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3030 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3031 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3032 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3033 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3034 |       }
3035 |     }
3036 |     return info;
3037 |   }

===== M0593 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.anonymous_L87.apply(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 88-117
Documentation:

Method code:
88 |       @Override
89 |       public boolean apply(Node n) {
90 |         // When the node is null it means, we reached the implicit return
91 |         // where the function returns (possibly without an return statement)
92 |         if (n == null) {
93 |           return false;
94 |         }
95 | 
96 |         // TODO(user): We only care about calls to functions that
97 |         // passes one of the dependent variable to a non-side-effect free
98 |         // function.
99 |         if (n.isCall() && NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
100 |           return true;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         if (n.isNew() && NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
104 |           return true;
105 |         }
106 | 
107 |         if (n.isDelProp()) {
108 |           return true;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
112 |           if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && apply(c)) {
113 |             return true;
114 |           }
115 |         }
116 |         return false;
117 |       }

104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0612 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1055-1057
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1055 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1056 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1057 |   }

===== M0625 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 639-666
Documentation:

Method code:
639 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
640 |     Node lastJump = null;
641 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
642 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
643 |       if (curHandler.isFunction()) {
644 |         break;
645 |       }
646 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
647 |         if (lastJump == null) {
648 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
649 |         } else {
650 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
651 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
652 |         }
653 |         lastJump = curHandler;
654 |       }
655 |     }
656 | 
657 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
658 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
659 |     }
660 | 
661 |     if (lastJump == null) {
662 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
663 |     } else {
664 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
665 |     }
666 |   }

===== M0631 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an
   *     IS_CONSTANT_NAME property.
   * <li>The normalize pass renames any variable with the IS_CONSTANT_NAME
   *     annotation and that is initialized to a constant value with
   *     a variable name including $$constant.

   * </ol>

===== M0641 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 910-931
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
921 |         return true;
922 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
923 |         return false;
924 |     }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0661 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 560-607
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
560 |     private void extractForInitializer(
561 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
562 | 
563 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
564 |         next = c.getNext();
565 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
566 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
567 |         switch (c.getType()) {
568 |           case Token.LABEL:
569 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
570 |             break;
571 |           case Token.FOR:
572 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
573 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
574 |               if (first.isVar()) {
575 |                 // Transform:
576 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
577 |                 // to:
578 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
579 |                 Node newStatement = first;
580 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
581 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
582 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
583 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
584 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
585 |               }
586 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
587 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
588 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
589 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
590 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
591 | 
592 |               Node newStatement;
593 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
594 |               // but are handled differently.
595 |               if (init.isVar()) {
596 |                 newStatement = init;
597 |               } else {
598 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
599 |               }
600 | 
601 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
602 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
603 |             }
604 |             break;
605 |         }
606 |       }
607 |     }

/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2665 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2666 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2667 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2668 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2669 |       return true;
2670 |     }
2671 | 
2672 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2673 |       return false;
2674 |     }
2675 | 
2676 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2677 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2678 |         return true;
2679 |       }
2680 |     }
2681 | 
2682 |     return false;
2683 |   }

===== M0692 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1407-1444
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1407 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1408 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1409 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1410 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1411 |           options.closurePass) {
1412 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1413 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1414 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1415 |           continue;
1416 |         }
1417 |       }
1418 | 
1419 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1420 | 
1421 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1422 |       if (n == null) {
1423 |         continue;
1424 |       }
1425 | 
1426 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1427 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1428 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1429 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1430 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1431 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1432 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1433 | 
1434 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1435 | 
1436 |         externs.add(input);
1437 |         staleInputs = true;
1438 |       }
1439 |     }
1440 | 
1441 |     if (staleInputs) {
1442 |       repartitionInputs();
1443 |     }
1444 |   }

===== M0694 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0695 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0704 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
390 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
391 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
392 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
393 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
394 | 
395 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
396 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
397 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
398 | 
399 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
400 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
401 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
402 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
403 |     }

406 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
407 |       if (superClass != null) {

409 |       }

413 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
414 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
415 |           }
416 |         }
417 |       }
418 |     }
419 | 
420 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
421 |       clearCachedValues();
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     return true;
425 |   }

===== M0718 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 790-934
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
790 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
791 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
792 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
793 |     // that we know to be safe
794 |     switch (n.getType()) {
795 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
796 |       case Token.AND:
797 |       case Token.BLOCK:
798 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
799 |       case Token.HOOK:
800 |       case Token.IF:
801 |       case Token.IN:
802 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
803 |       case Token.NUMBER:
804 |       case Token.OR:
805 |       case Token.THIS:
806 |       case Token.TRUE:
807 |       case Token.FALSE:
808 |       case Token.NULL:
809 |       case Token.STRING:
810 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
811 |       case Token.SWITCH:
812 |       case Token.TRY:
813 |       case Token.EMPTY:
814 |         break;
815 | 
816 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
817 |       case Token.THROW:
818 |         return true;
819 | 
820 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
821 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
822 |           return true;
823 |         }
824 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
825 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
826 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
827 |             return true;
828 |           }
829 |         }
830 |         return false;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
833 |       case Token.REGEXP:
834 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
840 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
841 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
842 |           return true;
843 |         }
844 |         break;
845 | 
846 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
847 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
848 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
849 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
850 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
851 | 
852 |       case Token.NEW:
853 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
854 |           return true;
855 |         }
856 | 
857 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
858 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
859 |           // side-effects
860 |           break;
861 |         }
862 |         return true;
863 | 
864 |       case Token.CALL:
865 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
866 |         // side effect property set.
867 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
868 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
869 |           // side-effects
870 |           break;
871 |         }
872 |         return true;
873 | 
874 |       default:
875 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
876 |           break;
877 |         }
878 | 
879 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
880 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
881 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
882 |             return true;
883 |           }
884 | 
885 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
886 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
887 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
888 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
889 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
890 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
891 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
892 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
893 |             return true;
894 |           }
895 | 
896 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
897 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
898 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
899 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
900 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
901 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
902 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
903 |               return false;
904 |             }
905 | 
906 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
907 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
908 |             // other objects.
909 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
910 |             // side-effect.
911 |             while (isGet(current)) {
912 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
913 |             }
914 | 
915 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
916 |           } else {
917 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
918 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
919 |             // preserve existing behavior.
920 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
921 |           }
922 |         }
923 | 
924 |         return true;
925 |     }
926 | 
927 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
928 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
929 |         return true;
930 |       }
931 |     }
932 | 
933 |     return false;
934 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0722 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 156-260
Documentation:

Method code:
156 |     @Override
157 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
158 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
159 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
160 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
161 |       if (inExterns && node.isName() && parent.isParamList()) {
162 |         return;
163 |       }
164 | 
165 |       Definition def =
166 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
167 |       if (def != null) {
168 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
169 |         if (name != null) {
170 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
171 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
172 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
173 |               !rValue.isFunction()) {
174 | 
175 |             // Unhandled complex expression
176 |             Definition unknownDef =
177 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
178 |             def = unknownDef;
179 |           }
180 | 
181 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
182 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
183 |           if (inExterns) {
184 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
185 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
186 | 
187 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
188 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
189 | 
190 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
191 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
192 |             // literal key.
193 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
194 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
195 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
196 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
197 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
198 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
199 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
200 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
201 |                   }
202 |                 }
203 |               }
204 | 
205 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
206 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
207 |               }
208 |             }
209 |           }
210 | 
211 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
212 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
213 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
214 |                                                    def,
215 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
216 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
217 |                                                    inExterns));
218 |         }
219 |       }
220 | 
221 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && parent.isExprResult()) {
222 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
223 |         if (name != null) {
224 | 
225 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
226 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
227 | 
228 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
229 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
230 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
231 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
232 | 
233 |           boolean dropStub = false;
234 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
235 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
236 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
237 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
238 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
239 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
240 |                   dropStub = true;
241 |                   break;
242 |                 }
243 |               }
244 |             }
245 |           }
246 | 
247 |           if (!dropStub) {
248 |             // Incomplete definition
249 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
250 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
251 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
252 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
253 |                                                      definition,
254 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
255 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
256 |                                                      inExterns));
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 |     }

1697 |       // need to clone them here.

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
797 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
798 |     if (item == null) {
799 |       return null;
800 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
801 |       return item.getNext();
802 |     } else {
803 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
804 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
805 |         return item.chain(result);
806 |       } else {
807 |         return item;
808 |       }
809 |     }
810 |   }

/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckPathsBetweenNodes.checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E>,DiGraphNode<N, E>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckPathsBetweenNodes.java
Original range: 199-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Verify that some non-looping paths from {@code a} to {@code b} pass
   * through at least one node where {@code nodePredicate} is true.
   */
Method code:
199 |   private boolean checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E> a,
200 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> b) {
201 |     if (nodePredicate.apply(a.getValue()) &&
202 |         (inclusive || (a != start && a != end))) {
203 |       return true;
204 |     }
205 |     if (a == b) {
206 |       return false;
207 |     }
208 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> e : a.getOutEdges()) {
209 |       // Once we visited that edge once, we no longer need to
210 |       // re-visit it again.
211 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == VISITED_EDGE) {
212 |         continue;
213 |       }
214 |       e.setAnnotation(VISITED_EDGE);
215 | 
216 |       if (ignoreEdge(e)) {
217 |         continue;
218 |       }
219 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == BACK_EDGE) {
220 |         continue;
221 |       }
222 | 
223 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> next = e.getDestination();
224 |       if (checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(next, b)) {
225 |         return true;
226 |       }
227 |     }
228 |     return false;
229 |   }

===== M0731 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 120-159
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
122 |     new NodeTraversal(
123 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
124 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
125 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
126 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
127 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
128 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
129 |     }
130 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
131 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
132 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
133 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
134 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
135 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
136 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
137 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
138 |     //   function f() {
139 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
140 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
141 |     //   }
142 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
143 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
144 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
145 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
146 |         .process(externs, root);
147 | 
148 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
149 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
150 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
151 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
152 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
153 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
157 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
158 |     }
159 |   }

===== M0735 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1198-1206
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1198 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1199 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1200 |       return -1;
1201 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1202 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1203 |     } else {
1204 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1205 |     }
1206 |   }

===== M0738 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
184 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

===== M0750 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 37-65
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
37 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
38 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
39 |     // can be simplified.
40 | 
41 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
42 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
43 |     if (parent == null) {
44 |       return null;
45 |     }
46 | 
47 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
48 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
49 |     } else if (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
50 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
51 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
52 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
53 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
54 |       }
55 |     } else if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
56 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
57 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
58 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
59 |           isExtern);
60 |     } else if (parent.isParamList()) {
61 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
62 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
63 |     }
64 |     return null;
65 |   }

===== M0755 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 490-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
490 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
491 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
492 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
493 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
494 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
495 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
496 |         String inputName = input.getName();
497 | 
498 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
499 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
500 |         // will get an error down the line.
501 |         inputs.add(input);
502 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return inputs;
506 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
508 |   private void validateTry(Node n) {
509 |     validateNodeType(Token.TRY, n);
510 |     validateMinimumChildCount(n, 2);
511 |     validateMaximumChildCount(n, 3);
512 |     validateBlock(n.getFirstChild());
513 | 
514 |     boolean seenCatchOrFinally = false;
515 | 
516 |     // Validate catch
517 |     Node catches = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
518 |     validateNodeType(Token.BLOCK, catches);
519 |     validateMaximumChildCount(catches, 1);
520 |     if (catches.hasChildren()) {
521 |       validateCatch(catches.getFirstChild());
522 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     // Validate finally
526 |     if (n.getChildCount() == 3) {
527 |       validateBlock(n.getLastChild());
528 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
529 |     }
530 | 
531 |     if (!seenCatchOrFinally) {
532 |       violation("Missing catch or finally for try statement.", n);
533 |     }
534 |   }

===== M0771 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 124-170
Documentation:

Method code:
124 |   @Override
125 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
126 | 
127 |     if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
128 |       return; // Don't even brother. All global variables are likely escaped.
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     if (LiveVariablesAnalysis.MAX_VARIABLES_TO_ANALYZE <
132 |         t.getScope().getVarCount()) {
133 |       return;
134 |     }
135 | 
136 |     // Compute the forward reaching definition.
137 |     ControlFlowAnalysis cfa = new ControlFlowAnalysis(compiler, false, true);
138 |     // Process the body of the function.
139 |     Preconditions.checkState(t.getScopeRoot().isFunction());
140 |     cfa.process(null, t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
141 |     cfg = cfa.getCfg();
142 |     reachingDef = new MustBeReachingVariableDef(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
143 |     reachingDef.analyze();
144 |     candidates = Lists.newLinkedList();
145 | 
146 |     // Using the forward reaching definition search to find all the inline
147 |     // candidates
148 |     new NodeTraversal(compiler, new GatherCandiates()).traverse(
149 |         t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
150 | 
151 |     // Compute the backward reaching use. The CFG can be reused.
152 |     reachingUses = new MaybeReachingVariableUse(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
153 |     reachingUses.analyze();
154 |     for (Candidate c : candidates) {
155 |       if (c.canInline()) {
156 |         c.inlineVariable();
157 | 
158 |         // If definition c has dependencies, then inlining it may have
159 |         // introduced new dependencies for our other inlining candidates.
160 |         //
161 |         // MustBeReachingVariableDef uses this dependency graph in its
162 |         // analysis, so some of these candidates may no longer be valid.
163 |         // We keep track of when the variable dependency graph changed
164 |         // so that we can back off appropriately.
165 |         if (!c.defMetadata.depends.isEmpty()) {
166 |           inlinedNewDependencies.add(t.getScope().getVar(c.varName));
167 |         }
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

===== M0774 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 495-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
495 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
496 |     switch (n.getType()) {
497 |       case Token.STRING:
498 |       case Token.NUMBER:
499 |       case Token.NULL:
500 |       case Token.TRUE:
501 |       case Token.FALSE:
502 |         return true;
503 |       case Token.NOT:
504 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
505 |       case Token.VOID:
506 |       case Token.NEG:
507 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
508 |       case Token.NAME:
509 |         String name = n.getString();
510 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
511 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
512 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
513 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
514 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
515 |     }
516 | 
517 |     return false;
518 |   }

===== M0777 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.

414 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0808 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 295-343
Documentation:

Method code:
295 |     @Override
296 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
297 |       if (n.isName()) {
298 |         String name = n.getString();
299 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
300 |           return;
301 |         }
302 | 
303 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
304 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
305 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
306 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
307 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
308 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
309 |             expectedConst = true;
310 |           } else {
311 |             expectedConst = false;
312 | 
313 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
314 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
315 |             if (var != null) {
316 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
317 |             }
318 | 
319 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
320 |               expectedConst = true;
321 |             } else {
322 |               expectedConst = false;
323 |             }
324 |           }
325 | 
326 |           if (expectedConst) {
327 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
328 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
329 |           } else {
330 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
331 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
332 |           }
333 |         }
334 | 
335 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
336 |         if (value == null) {
337 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
338 |         } else {
339 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
340 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
341 |         }
342 |       }
343 |     }

55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

===== M0820 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0828 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
716 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
717 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
718 |         "The new child node has siblings.");

===== M0855 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

===== M0865 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

===== M0879 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 135-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
135 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
136 |     if (node.isName()) {
137 |       String name = node.getString();
138 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
139 |         return name;
140 |       } else {
141 |         return null;
142 |       }
143 |     } else if (node.isGetProp()) {
144 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
145 |     }
146 |     return null;
147 |   }

/**
   * Computes all the local variables that rValue reads from and store that
   * in the def's depends set.

===== M0885 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processTryStatement(TryStatement)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 985-1018
Documentation:

Method code:
985 |     @Override
986 |     Node processTryStatement(TryStatement statementNode) {
987 |       Node node = newNode(Token.TRY,
988 |           transformBlock(statementNode.getTryBlock()));
989 |       Node block = newNode(Token.BLOCK);
990 |       boolean lineSet = false;
991 | 
992 |       for (CatchClause cc : statementNode.getCatchClauses()) {
993 |         // Mark the enclosing block at the same line as the first catch
994 |         // clause.
995 |         if (lineSet == false) {
996 |           block.setLineno(cc.getLineno());
997 |           maybeSetLengthFrom(block, cc);
998 |           lineSet = true;
999 |         }
1000 |         block.addChildToBack(transform(cc));
1001 |       }
1002 |       node.addChildToBack(block);
1003 | 
1004 |       AstNode finallyBlock = statementNode.getFinallyBlock();
1005 |       if (finallyBlock != null) {
1006 |         node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(finallyBlock));
1007 |       }
1008 | 
1009 |       // If we didn't set the line on the catch clause, then
1010 |       // we've got an empty catch clause.  Set its line to be the same
1011 |       // as the finally block (to match Old Rhino's behavior.)
1012 |       if ((lineSet == false) && (finallyBlock != null)) {
1013 |         block.setLineno(finallyBlock.getLineno());
1014 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(block, finallyBlock);
1015 |       }
1016 | 
1017 |       return node;
1018 |     }

===== M0890 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

===== M0897 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MustBeReachingVariableDef.addToDefIfLocal(String,Node,Node,MustDef)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MustBeReachingVariableDef.java
Original range: 327-358
Documentation:
/**
   * Set the variable lattice for the given name to the node value in the def
   * lattice. Do nothing if the variable name is one of the escaped variable.
   *
   * @param node The CFG node where the definition should be record to.
   *     {@code null} if this is a conditional define.
   */
Method code:
327 |   private void addToDefIfLocal( String name, @Nullable Node node,
328 |       @Nullable Node rValue, MustDef def) {
329 |     Var var = jsScope.getVar(name);
330 | 
331 |     // var might be null because the variable might be defined in the extern
332 |     // that we might not traverse.
333 |     if (var == null || var.scope != jsScope) {
334 |       return;
335 |     }
336 | 
337 |     for (Var other : def.reachingDef.keySet()) {
338 |       Definition otherDef = def.reachingDef.get(other);
339 |       if (otherDef == null) {
340 |         continue;
341 |       }
342 |       if (otherDef.depends.contains(var)) {
343 |         def.reachingDef.put(other, null);
344 |       }
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     if (!escaped.contains(var)) {
348 |       if (node == null) {
349 |         def.reachingDef.put(var, null);
350 |       } else {
351 |         Definition definition = new Definition(node);
352 |         if (rValue != null) {
353 |           computeDependence(definition, rValue);
354 |         }
355 |         def.reachingDef.put(var, definition);
356 |       }
357 |     }
358 |   }

633 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
634 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
635 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
636 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
637 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
638 |       if (typeSet == null) {

640 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
641 |       }

647 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {

649 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);

651 |     }

===== M0931 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

   * Initializes the work list and the control flow graph.

Method code:
248 |   protected void initialize() {
249 |     // TODO(user): Calling clear doesn't deallocate the memory in a
250 |     // LinkedHashSet. Consider creating a new work set if we plan to repeatedly
251 |     // call analyze.

255 |           createInitialEstimateLattice()));

===== M0934 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateMinimumChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 789-804
Documentation:

Method code:
789 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
790 |     boolean valid = false;
791 |     if (i == 1) {
792 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
793 |     } else if (i == 2) {
794 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
795 |     } else {
796 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
797 |     }
798 | 
799 |     if (!valid) {
800 |       violation(
801 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
802 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
803 |     }
804 |   }
